TL;DR: 提出了HDS（整体数据调度器），一个基于强化学习（SAC算法）的在线数据混合框架，通过多目标奖励函数（数据质量、跨域影响、模型权重范数）来动态调整LLM预训练的数据组成。在The Pile基准上，HDS以比次优方法少44%的训练迭代达到相同验证困惑度，并在MMLU 0-shot任务上提升7.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线数据混合方法仅从单一优化视角出发，忽视了LLM预训练需要从多个维度考虑动态数据组成的需求。

Method: 将数据调度问题建模为连续控制空间中的强化学习问题，使用SAC算法探索高维策略空间；设计了一个多目标整体奖励函数，整合了数据驱动奖励（质量）、损失驱动奖励（跨域影响）和模型驱动奖励（权重范数）三个视角。

Result: 在The Pile上，HDS以少44%的训练迭代达到次优方法的最终验证困惑度；MMLU 0-shot任务提升7.2%，其他基准上也有一致提升。

Conclusion: HDS通过多维度、多目标的在线数据混合策略，能够显著提升LLM预训练的效率与最终模型能力。

Abstract: The composition of training data, governed by the diversity of sources and their mixing strategy, is a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training. Online Data Mixing (ODM), the technique of adaptively adjusting data mixtures during training, has emerged as a promising direction to improve efficiency. However, existing methods are constrained by their reliance on a singular optimization perspective, which fundamentally overlooks the need for complex LLM pre-training to consider the dynamic data composition from multiple dimensions. To overcome this limitation, we introduce the Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS), a novel online data mixing framework. HDS formulates the data scheduling challenge as a reinforcement learning problem in a continuous control space and leverages the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm for its stability and sample efficiency in exploring the high-dimensional policy space. At the core of HDS lies a novel multi-objective, holistic reward function that integrates three critical perspectives: a data-driven reward for quality, a loss-driven reward capturing inter-domain influence, and a model-driven reward based on weight norms. To validate our design and determine its optimal configuration, we conducted systematic experiments on LLMs of various sizes. On The Pile benchmark, HDS reaches the final validation perplexity of the next best method with 44% fewer training iterations. Furthermore, it achieves a 7.2% improvement on the MMLU 0-shot task along with consistent gains on other benchmarks, showcasing its ability to enhance both training efficiency and final model capability.

</details>


### [180] [A Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation Sampler for Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24140)
*Feiyang Fu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种时间重参数化累积强度外推（TR-CIE）采样器，通过时间重参数化和累积强度外推更新规则，在有限函数评估次数下提升离散流匹配的采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配（DFM）在离散状态空间生成建模中表现良好，但实践中常用的τ-leaping离散化采样方法在有限函数评估次数（NFE）下效率不足，缺乏高效的采样策略。

Method: TR-CIE由两部分组成：(1) 基于噪声调度的时间重参数化，重新缩放时间网格，吸收调度相关的增长项并缓解终端采样阶段的刚性问题；(2) 累积强度外推更新规则，复用上一步缓存的模型输出作为历史项，改进非均匀时间网格上的逐步累积强度近似。

Result: 在合成任务、文本生成和文本到图像基准上的实验表明，TR-CIE在有限NFE下提升了采样质量。每步仅需一次函数评估，相较于标准τ-leaping采样器未引入额外模型评估。

Conclusion: TR-CIE提供了一种高效且无需额外计算成本的DFM采样改进方案，理论分析了累积强度的局部逼近误差并建立了收敛性结果。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $τ$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $τ$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>


### [181] [AsyncOPD: How Stale Can On-Policy Distillation Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24143)
*Wonjun Kang,Kevin Galim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjun Kang,Sanghyun Park,Donghoon Kim,Minjae Lee,Minseo Kim,Rishabh Tiwari,Yuchen Zeng,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次系统研究异步on-policy蒸馏中的数据陈旧性问题，提出了AsyncOPD训练框架，在保持精度的同时将训练吞吐量提升1.6-3.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy蒸馏面临on-policy系统瓶颈（rollout生成耗时），异步训练可解耦rollout与学习但引入陈旧策略数据，该问题在异步RL中有研究但在OPD中尚未探索。

Method: 1) 分析KL方向对陈旧数据鲁棒性的影响（前向KL鲁棒，反向KL脆弱）；2) 比较异步RL稳定化方法在OPD中的效果，发现反向KL下重计算损失信号更优；3) 研究有限教师分数缓存的偏差-方差权衡，提出多样本蒙特卡洛估计；4) 构建AsyncOPD异步训练流水线。

Result: AsyncOPD在多种推理任务上实现1.6倍到3.8倍的训练吞吐量提升，同时达到与严格同步训练相当的精度。

Conclusion: 异步OPD可有效缓解on-policy系统瓶颈，KL方向选择至关重要，基于当前学生重计算损失信号是处理陈旧性的有效手段，多样本MC估计在有限缓存下表现良好。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts guided by teacher feedback and is becoming increasingly important for large language model (LLM) post-training. Like reinforcement learning (RL), however, OPD faces an on-policy systems bottleneck, as rollouts can dominate training time for reasoning workloads. Asynchronous training pipelines can alleviate this bottleneck by decoupling rollout generation from learner updates, but doing so introduces stale-policy data. While prior work has studied stale data in asynchronous RL, its effects in OPD remain underexplored. We present the first systematic study of staleness in asynchronous OPD, focusing on a practical setting where teacher feedback is implemented through local KL losses and full-vocabulary teacher logits are too expensive to store or transfer, necessitating finite teacher-score caches. We first show that KL direction changes the stale-data problem: teacher-weighted forward KL is more robust to stale rollouts, whereas student-weighted reverse KL is vulnerable. Second, for this vulnerable reverse-KL case, we study whether methods designed to stabilize asynchronous RL can mitigate OPD staleness. In our experiments, they do not improve over a simpler OPD-specific surrogate: recomputing the reverse-KL signal under the current student at learner time. Third, we analyze how finite teacher-score caches create a bias-variance tradeoff for sparse and sampled reverse-KL OPD estimators. This motivates multi-sample Monte Carlo (MC), which preserves MC correctability while reducing one-sample variance. Finally, we present and open-source AsyncOPD, a fully asynchronous OPD training pipeline built from these estimator choices. Experiments show that AsyncOPD improves training throughput by $1.6\times$ to $3.8\times$ over strict synchronous training while reaching comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [182] [Lightweight Transformer Models for On-Device Fault Detection: A Benchmark Study on Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24173)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在三个公开数据集上对比了传统机器学习方法（随机森林、XGBoost、SVM、逻辑回归）与轻量级Transformer架构（DistilBERT、TinyBERT、MobileBERT）在设备端故障检测任务中的表现。结果显示：在数据分离良好的场景下，轻量级Transformer可匹配传统方法的F1分数（87.8%），但模型体积大100倍、延迟高9000倍；TinyBERT-4L是最适合部署的Transformer模型（55MB, 18ms）；INT8量化可缩减25%体积并保持86.9% F1；自适应推理管线（97.9%由量化轻量模型处理，2.1%由大模型处理）达到87.6% F1和19.5ms平均延迟；在严重不平衡数据集上，所有方法均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的硬件上部署机器学习模型进行实时故障检测，需要在精度、延迟和模型大小之间进行权衡，但缺乏对传统ML方法与轻量级Transformer在该场景下的系统性对比基准。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS、SECOM和UCI AI4I 2020三个公开数据集上，对传统ML方法（Random Forest、XGBoost、SVM、Logistic Regression）与轻量级Transformer（DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT）进行二分类故障检测对比评估，并进一步评估INT8动态量化和两阶段自适应推理管线。

Result: 在C-MAPSS上轻量级Transformer达到87.8% F1（与传统ML相当），但模型大小和延迟劣势显著；TinyBERT-4L最适于部署（55MB, 18ms）；INT8量化缩减25%体积；自适应管线达到87.6% F1和19.5ms延迟；在不平衡数据集上所有方法均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 对于设备端故障检测，传统ML方法在精度-效率权衡上仍优于轻量级Transformer；自适应推理管线可缓解部分效率问题；极端类别不平衡是当前所有方法的根本性挑战。

Abstract: On-device fault detection enables real-time diagnostics without cloud dependency, but deploying machine learning models on resource-constrained hardware demands careful tradeoffs between accuracy, latency, and model size. We present a benchmark comparing traditional ML methods (Random Forest, XGBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression) against lightweight transformer architectures (DistilBERT, TinyBERT-6L, TinyBERT-4L, MobileBERT) for binary fault detection across three public datasets: NASA C-MAPSS turbofan degradation, SECOM semiconductor manufacturing, and UCI AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance. We evaluate classification performance (F1-score, AUC), model size, and CPU inference latency, and further assess INT8 dynamic quantization and a two-stage adaptive inference pipeline. Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [183] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 介绍Ariadne，一种解码器-only的路线生成器，通过提示-补全序列统一表示目标分子、约束条件和合成路线，在多个基准上优于或媲美现有方法，并将序列生成从专用逆合成模型扩展到提示条件化的结构路线生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆合成规划方法中，经典规划器需要迭代应用单步反应模型且受约束时需专用算法，而直接路线生成方法仍为不同规划规格训练独立模型，缺乏统一的灵活框架。

Method: 提出Ariadne，一个解码器-only的路线生成器，将目标分子、可选约束和合成路线编码为一个提示-补全序列进行统一建模，用一个24层检查点支持多种约束条件下的路线生成。

Result: 在RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160基准上，添加深度约束提示使Solv-0提升13.7点，添加所需叶节点约束提升31.2点；在所需叶节点约束下Top-10和Solv-0优于DESP，推理时间仅24 GPU分钟对比6.8 GPU小时；标准重构任务中与DMS Explorer XL相当但推理时间减半；在路线留出重构上增益最明显，但AiZynthFinder MCTS在多组Solv-0对比中仍更强。

Conclusion: Ariadne将序列生成从专用逆合成模型扩展到提示条件化的结构路线生成，代码已开源，但Tier-1--3路线检查器仍是模型对实验化学家有用的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>


### [184] [Managing Task Execution for Unknown Workloads in Batteryless IoT: A Hardware-Agnostic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24340)
*Samer Nasser,Henrique Duarte Moura,Ritesh Kumar Singh,Maarten Weyn,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对无电池物联网系统能量高度波动的问题，提出两种硬件无关的动态调度策略（强化学习与近似预测方法），与传统方案在不同容量场景下的性能权衡分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统能量感知调度器依赖静态执行阈值或硬件特定预测量，难以应对无电池IoT系统中高度不确定的工作负载；现有方案缺乏通用性，亟需硬件无关的智能调度策略。

Method: 提出两种硬件无关的黑盒动态调度方法：无模型强化学习代理（RL）和即时近似预测方法（AP）；在基于真实太阳辐射数据和动态LoRa传输轮廓的物理精确仿真框架中，与自适应任务速率方法（AsTAR）及优化静态阈值进行对比评估。

Result: AP方法提供轻量级接近理论最优的任务吞吐量；RL代理实现可调谐的存活-执行平衡；AsTAR在长能量间隙中擅长执行节奏控制；大容量设备可依赖简单静态策略。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优策略；小电容严重受限系统需高级调度策略增强弹性，大能量缓冲设备可选用计算开销更低的静态策略。

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>


### [185] [Parallel Manifold Steering: Efficient Adaptation of Large Associative Memories via Residual Energy Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24396)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出H-Res（层次化残差引导）方法，通过在激活流形上学习状态依赖的向量场来引导Transformer的注意力动力学进入任务特定吸引子盆地，在不修改权重或增加序列长度的前提下实现冻结大模型的高效适配，解决了塑性-稳定性困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大Transformer模型作为稠密联想记忆（DAM）运行，通过自注意力机制的高维吸引子动力学检索知识。然而，将冻结的记忆系统适配到新任务面临根本性的"塑性-稳定性"困境：现有方法如LoRA直接修改突触权重存在灾难性干扰风险，而VPT等提示方法则因静态提示令牌堵塞检索缓冲区而降低联想能力。

Method: 将适配问题形式化为激活流形上的控制问题，学习一个状态依赖的向量场（层次化残差引导），该向量场将令牌轨迹引导至任务特定的吸引子盆地，同时严格证明该方法保持基础模型的注意力熵并促进神经坍缩。

Result: 在联想检索任务上比全局权重修改方法提升26%的性能，消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，并能有效扩展到结构化领域。

Conclusion: H-Res通过在不改变全局平衡态或扩展序列长度的情况下调控Transformer的有效能量景观，实现了更优的冻结模型适配策略。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction. We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [186] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自然标识符（NIDs）作为新型解决方案，利用LLM训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、短链接）来支持事后差分隐私审计和数据集推断，无需重新训练或私有保留数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计差分隐私的方法需在训练中插入特制金丝雀数据，无法审计已训练模型；数据集推断需访问同分布的私有非成员保留数据集，而该数据集在实际中难以获取。这些限制严重阻碍了可扩展的事后审计。

Method: 利用LLM训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（NIDs，如加密哈希和短链接），其格式特性允许生成无限的同分布随机字符串，既可作为审计用的替代金丝雀，也可作为数据集推断的同分布保留数据。

Result: 实验表明，使用NIDs可以在无需重新训练的情况下实现事后差分隐私审计，并能在任何包含NIDs的可疑数据集上进行数据集推断，无需私有非成员保留数据集。

Conclusion: NIDs有效地解决了现有隐私审计方法的两大瓶颈——无法审计已训练模型和缺乏同分布保留数据集，为LLM的可扩展事后审计提供了切实可行的方案。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [187] [Data Augmentation: A Fourier Analysis Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24418)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过傅里叶分析和有限群表示论，证明了在广泛的学习问题中，基于随机采样子集的局部数据增强能够在近似误差可忽略的前提下达到与全局增强相同的统计最优率，同时证明了精确不变性必须通过全群平均实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强虽能利用对称性提升泛化性能，但当群规模较大时，全群增强的计算成本过高，因此核心问题是：局部数据增强能否在统计上（泛化能力和样本复杂度）达到与全局增强相同的效果？

Method: 利用傅里叶分析和有限群表示论建立统一理论框架，从统计学习理论和信息论角度分析局部增强与全局增强的统计性能差异。

Result: 局部数据增强（随机采样子集）在广泛经典学习问题中可实现与全群增强相同的极小化最优率，近似误差随子集规模增大而消失；同时，精确不变性要求全群平均，无法被任何严格子集替代。

Conclusion: 该研究统一了全局与局部数据增强、精确与近似对称性约束的理论视角，为计算可扩展的统计最优学习提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a simple and model-agnostic approach for exploiting known invariances in learning problems. Given a group acting on the input space, one augments the training set with transformed copies of each sample. Because it exploits symmetries without modifying the underlying learning algorithm, data augmentation can be applied broadly across learning methods. However, this universality comes at a computational cost: when the group is large, full group-sized augmentation quickly becomes computationally infeasible. This raises a fundamental question: Can partial data augmentation achieve the same statistical benefits as full augmentation in terms of generalization and sample complexity? We develop a general framework for investigating this question using Fourier analysis and the representation theory of finite groups. We show that, for a broad class of classical learning problems, partial data augmentation based on a randomly sampled subset of group elements achieves the same minimax rates as full augmentation, up to an approximation error that vanishes as the subset size increases. Our results provide a theoretical explanation for why partial augmentation can retain the statistical benefits of full augmentation despite enforcing symmetry only approximately, and shed light on a recently raised question in learning with symmetries: whether statistically optimal learning under general group invariances can be achieved using computationally scalable methods. Moreover, we prove a complementary impossibility result: enforcing exact invariance via data augmentation requires averaging over the entire group, and cannot be achieved by any strict subset when the hypothesis space is sufficiently expressive. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on full and partial data augmentation, as well as exact and approximate symmetry enforcement.

</details>


### [188] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，采用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer处理轴承故障诊断中数据集异质性、工况变化和标签稀缺的并发挑战，在仅10%目标标签数据下达到92.61%平均准确率，超越SOTA方法17.24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法孤立处理数据集异质性、工况变化和标签稀缺问题，依赖隐式特征对齐，在并发挑战下效果有限。

Method: 提出知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架：1）轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer（因果自注意力）用于层次化特征提取；2）预训练编码器权重和故障原型嵌入作为知识载体，实现多源预训练到目标域适应；3）多源学习学习通用表示，原型知识调制进行目标适应，分类学自适应分类实现异构故障类别迁移。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，仅用10%目标标签数据达到92.61%平均准确率，超越SOTA方法17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业4.0应用中的经济高效预测性维护提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [189] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对9种面向节点属性预测的图基础模型（GFMs）进行了公平且严格的重新评估，发现只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新GFM在预测性能上优于调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFMs）在节点属性预测任务中受到广泛关注，但该领域尚未形成统一的评估标准，不同工作采用差异巨大的评估方式，阻碍了GFMs之间以及与其他模型的可比性。

Method: 对9种近期提出的节点属性预测GFMs进行公平且严格的重新评估，并与强图神经网络（GNN）基线进行对比。

Result: 在评估的GFMs中，只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新模型在预测性能上优于调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。

Conclusion: 该领域需要更统一的评估协议，以促进GFMs的可靠比较与发展。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [190] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从集体计算视角重新审视LLM，将其视为高维稠密联想记忆网络，提出基于能量景观和吉布斯采样的检索机制，通过谱熵加权多路径推理实现向吸引子盆地的动态弛豫，在GSM8K上提升Phi-3.5性能5.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统自回归生成视角，从联想记忆与能量景观的角度理解LLM的推理机制，解释幻觉产生的几何根源，并利用物理启发方法提升推理鲁棒性。

Method: 将数学推理建模为高维稠密联想记忆中的能量景观：正确推理链对应平坦吸引子盆地（平最小值），幻觉对应尖锐不稳定局部最小值。提出基于轨迹谱熵的吉布斯测度检索机制，对多条推理路径按逆能量加权采样（$P \propto e^{-βE}$），近似联想记忆的平衡分布，实现系统向稳健解的弛豫。

Result: 在Microsoft Phi-3.5模型上，GSM8K准确率从84.7%提升至90.1%，提升幅度5.38%。

Conclusion: 推理更应被建模为向吸引子盆地动态沉降的过程，而非贪婪的逐词预测，物理启发的弛豫机制能有效提升LLM推理质量。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [191] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QC-SMOTE，一种基于复合邻域可信度评分的质量受控过采样框架，通过IPQ引导的最佳K策略生成合成样本，并在噪声区域回退为复制原始样本。在30个不平衡数据集上取得了最优的AUC-ROC和Macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMOTE等现有过采样方法在噪声或类别重叠区域容易生成低质量合成样本，需要一种质量可控的过采样方法来提高合成样本的可靠性。

Method: 构建复合邻域可信度评分（结合局部密度、安全水平和与多数类的隔离度）评估少数类样本可靠性；采用IPQ引导的最佳K策略评估中点纯度并在必要时进行多数类清除；生成行为根据重叠-不平衡 regimes 自适应调整插值范围和选择标准；低质量合成样本在邻域纯度低于自适应阈值时被替换为原始少数类副本。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上使用重复分层交叉验证，QC-SMOTE在AUC-ROC和Macro F1指标上达到最强平均性能，在中度和严重不平衡条件下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 质量感知、几何自适应的合成采样对于鲁棒的不平衡分类至关重要；在噪声严重区域回退为复制策略提供了优雅降级机制。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [192] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe提出了一种基于条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）的自动化机器学习数据准备流水线合成框架，通过将流水线构建建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成，使用轨迹平衡目标、深度语义调制（FiLM）和失败感知流目标，在74个真实数据集上平均提升准确率11.96%，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA的Multi-DQN方法存在三个关键局限：解耦的值估计器削弱了长期信用分配、数据集上下文仅被弱注入策略、稀疏搜索空间中存在大量无效状态导致探索效率低下。因此需要一种能够有效处理长序列信用分配、充分利用数据集语义信息并高效探索搜索空间的新方法。

Method: 将流水线合成建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成；使用C-GFlowNets结合Trajectory Balance目标连接终端验证奖励与早期决策；通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM)引入LLM推导的逻辑先验，根据数据集语义调节策略内部激活；在流目标中融入失败感知机制以避免无效状态，将搜索集中在高潜力区域。

Result: 在两个基准套件（74个真实数据集）上，FlowPipe优于SOTA基线，平均准确率提升11.96%，训练收敛速度达到12.5倍加速。

Conclusion: FlowPipe通过统一的生成流框架有效解决了自动化数据准备流水线合成中的信用分配、语义注入和无效状态探索三大挑战，显著提升了性能与效率。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [193] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Grad Detect是一种基于梯度分析的幻觉检测方法，通过单次前向-反向传播获取层级梯度模式来预测LLM输出是否正确，在多个基准上优于置信度方法和采样方法，且发现最后5层集中了97%以上的判别信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成过程中容易产生幻觉，现有检测方法主要依赖输出级信号（如置信度或采样），忽略了模型内部梯度结构蕴含的丰富信息。作者希望利用梯度信息来更准确地预测幻觉。

Method: 提出Grad Detect方法，在推理过程中进行一次前向-反向传播，分析各层的梯度模式来预测输出正确性。对11个模型（来自4个架构家族）进行全面的层级消融实验。

Result: 在多个Q&A基准上的幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务中，Grad Detect一致优于置信度基线和采样基线；最后5层集中了超过97%的判别梯度信号，可高效部署。

Conclusion: Grad Detect提供了一个统一的框架来预测LLM可靠性的多个维度，兼具强预测性能和可解释性，能揭示模型故障的来源位置。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [194] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hartley神经算子（HNO），作为傅里叶神经算子（FNO）的纯实数版本，用离散Hartley变换替代FFT以消除复数运算的冗余。研究发现，对于自伴椭圆算子（如泊松、双调和方程），HNO表现更优，因为其格林函数是实对称的；而对于含相位的时间相关算子（如波动、对流、Burgers和Navier-Stokes方程），FNO更受青睐。热方程作为无相位的边界情况，两者表现相当。结论是应匹配谱基与算子对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO在复傅里叶域进行全局卷积参数化，对于实值PDE解存在共轭对称带来的表示冗余。本文旨在消除这种冗余，提出纯实数的替代方案，并探究不同谱基对各类PDE算子的适应性。

Method: 提出Hartley神经算子（HNO），使用纯实的离散Hartley变换替代FFT，每个保留谱模式只学习一个实数权重，无复数运算。HNO与FNO在等宽度条件下参数数量相同，仅谱基不同。在多种PDE类别、初值族和边界条件下进行相同设置的训练和基准测试。

Result: 发现椭圆型方程（泊松、双调和）与时间依赖型方程（波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）之间存在明确的分界线，且该分界线随算子相位含量单调变化，与格林函数理论分析一致。热方程是边界情况，两者表现相近。

Conclusion: 不存在普适最优的神经算子，应预测性地将谱基与解算子的对称性匹配：实对称格林函数对应HNO，含相位算子对应FNO。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>

### [195] [Hessian-augmented Supervised Learning for Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23827)
*Matías Gómez-Aedo,Behzad Azmi,Yuyang Huang,Dante Kalise,Karl Kunisch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动方法，通过Pontryagin极大值原理生成包含值函数、梯度、Hessian的训练数据，利用加权最小二乘回归在稀疏多项式基上近似值函数，并验证了二阶数据增强能显著降低样本复杂度并提升闭环控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维确定性最优控制问题中，传统值函数近似方法面临样本复杂度高、计算代价大的挑战，需要更高效的数据驱动方法来降低训练样本需求。

Method: 1) 从多个初始条件求解Pontryagin极大值原理最优性系统生成训练数据；2) 沿最优轨迹通过矩阵Riccati方程获取Hessian信息；3) 在双曲交叉指标集的稀疏多项式基上进行加权最小二乘回归，梯度与Hessian贡献额外线性方程；4) 从学习到的值函数解析地恢复反馈律；5) 高维场景采用部分Hessian策略控制数据生成成本。

Result: 二阶数据增强显著提高了近似精度和闭环性能；相比低阶方法，训练样本数量减少了一个数量级；在高维问题上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 值函数的梯度与Hessian信息可大幅降低样本复杂度，是一种高效的高维最优控制值函数近似方法。

Abstract: A data-driven method is developed for approximating value functions in deterministic optimal control problems with nonlinear control-affine dynamics. The Pontryagin Maximum Principle optimality system is solved from multiple initial conditions to generate training data consisting of values, gradients, and Hessians of the value function, where Hessian information is obtained from a matrix Riccati equation along optimal trajectories. These quantities augment a weighted least-squares regression over sparse polynomial bases on hyperbolic cross index sets, with gradients and Hessians contributing additional linear equations per sample and substantially reducing sample complexity compared to value-only regression. Feedback laws are recovered analytically from the learned value function. In high dimensions, a partial Hessian strategy controls the cost of data generation. The approach is validated on problems of increasing state dimension, where second-order data augmentation is shown to improve approximation accuracy and closed-loop performance, with up to an order-of-magnitude reduction in the number of training samples required relative to lower-order methods.

</details>


### [196] [Computational Framework for Bézier Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23841)
*Esteban Leiva,Andrés L. Medaglia,Luis F. Zuluaga*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于一阶优化方法的Bézier分布拟合计算框架，利用保序回归实现高效投影算子，将计算速度提升3-4个数量级，并开发了开源Python包bezierv。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bézier分布虽能灵活建模复杂形状的有界连续单变量分布，但因缺乏高效拟合程序和现代软件实现而应用受限。

Method: 采用最小误差和最大似然估计，利用一阶优化方法并利用参数空间几何结构；通过保序回归构建可行集的渐进无损凸限制，实现高效投影算子。

Result: 相比传统无导数方法，计算速度提升3-4个数量级；相比非线性求解器IPOPT，平均快3个数量级且更鲁棒，精度相当。

Conclusion: 所提框架显著提升了Bézier分布的拟合效率与鲁棒性，开源的bezierv包弥合了理论与实践的差距。

Abstract: Flexible continuous univariate distributions with bounded support are essential for accurate input modeling in stochastic simulation and decision analysis. Although Bézier distributions provide a powerful family capable of representing complex shapes, their adoption has been hindered by the lack of efficient fitting procedures and modern software implementations. This paper develops a computational framework for fitting Bézier distributions to empirical data via both minimum error and maximum likelihood estimation, leveraging first-order optimization methods and exploiting the geometry of the parameter space. We identify provably (asymptotically) lossless convex restrictions of the feasible set that enable efficient projection operators based on isotonic regression and develop first-order algorithms that reduce computational runtime by three to four orders of magnitude compared to traditional derivative-free methods, while delivering consistent fits across real-world data. When benchmarked against the nonlinear solver IPOPT, our methods prove three orders of magnitude faster on average and more robust, while achieving comparable accuracy. To bridge the gap between theory and practice, we introduce bezierv, an open-source Python package providing a unified interface for fitting, analyzing, and convolving Bézier distributions.

</details>


### [197] [Constrained Variable Projection for Structured Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23939)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Sara Venturini,Francesco Rinaldi,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将变量投影技术扩展为受约束的双层优化框架，用于可分离非线性最小二乘问题，并设计了与自动微分兼容的条件梯度算法，在稀疏自编码、字典学习、盲反卷积和少样本学习等任务上展示了相比联合优化更高的计算效率和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变量投影技术用于可分离非线性最小二乘问题，但缺乏对剩余变量施加凸约束以及处理数据科学模型中结构化下层变量的能力。作者希望将变量投影扩展为更一般的双层优化框架，以适用于带约束的数据科学模型。

Method: 将变量投影解释为折叠的双层优化问题，推导出与自动微分兼容的精确简化梯度公式，提出用于受约束简化问题的条件梯度算法，并讨论了结构化下层变量的扩展。

Result: 在稀疏自编码、字典学习、盲反卷积和少样本学习的数值实验中，该方法相比联合优化基线在壁钟效率和数据效率上均有提升。

Conclusion: 在标准光滑性和紧致性假设下建立了收敛保证，实验表明所提方法在实际数据科学任务中有效。

Abstract: Variable projection is a classical technique for separable nonlinear least-squares problems, in which variables that enter linearly are eliminated exactly, yielding a reduced nonlinear problem. By expressing this framework as a particular instance of a broader class of bilevel optimization problems, we develop a constrained variable-projection framework for data-science models, where the remaining variables are subject to convex constraints and the eliminated variables arise from a lower-level least-squares problem. In particular, by interpreting variable projection as a collapsed bilevel optimization problem, we derive exact reduced-gradient formulas compatible with automatic differentiation and propose a conditional-gradient algorithm for the resulting constrained reduced problem. We establish convergence guarantees under standard smoothness and compactness assumptions, and discuss extensions to structured lower-level variables. Numerical experiments on sparse autoencoding, dictionary learning, blind deconvolution, and few-shot learning suggest that the method can improve wall-clock efficiency and data efficiency relative to natural joint-optimization baselines.

</details>


### [198] [A Lorentzian Lasry-Lions regularization theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23976)
*Alec Metsch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了洛伦兹时空中的Lasry-Lions正则化定理，证明了在全局双曲时空中，若函数u的前向Lax-Oleinik演化在局部具有半凹性和类时超微分，则其复合函数具有C^{1,1}_{loc}正则性，并应用于最优传输问题中位移插值中间测度的对偶最大化对的正则性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洛伦兹几何与最优传输的交叉领域，需要建立一种正则化机制，使得定义在全局双曲时空上的函数通过Lax-Oleinik演化后能够获得更好的光滑性，从而为后续应用提供理论保障。

Method: 采用Lorentzian Lasry-Lions正则化方法，通过前向Lax-Oleinik演化算子的局部半凹性假设和未来指向类时超微分存在性条件，构造复合算子并证明其C^{1,1}_{loc}正则性。

Result: 证明了在全局双曲时空中，满足局部半凹性和类时超微分条件的函数经过正则化后可达C^{1,1}_{loc}类；在最优传输中，位移插值的任意中间测度间存在C^{1,1}_{loc}正则的对偶最大化对。

Conclusion: 该定理推广了经典的Lasry-Lions正则化结果到洛伦兹设定，并为最优传输中的对偶正则性提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: The main goal of this paper is to establish a general Lorentzian Lasry-Lions regularization theorem: let $u$ be a function defined on a globally hyperbolic spacetime. Assume that its forward Lax--Oleinik evolution $Tu$ is locally semiconcave in a neighbourhood of $(t_0,y_0)$ and has future-directed timelike superdifferentials there. Then, for $t$ close to $t_0$ and sufficiently small $s>0$, the function $\hat T_s\circ T_tu$ is of class $C_{\mathrm{loc}}^{1,1}$ in a neighbourhood of $y_0$. We give sufficient conditions ensuring the assumptions of the theorem and present an application to optimal transport: under quite general assumptions, for any two intermediate measures along a displacement interpolation, there exists a $C^{1,1}_{loc}$-regular maximizing pair in the dual formulation.

</details>


### [199] [Traveling profiles and control cost for a PDE describing the evolution of invasive species](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23979)
*Stefano Bianchini,Chiara Trifone*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对入侵物种控制模型，分析了最优行进波的结构，证明了最优控制剖面的唯一性、显式一阶条件、控制的可积性与光滑性，并刻画了最小控制努力函数E(β,f)的凸性、正则性和渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为入侵物种控制模型中的行进波提供严格的最优性分析，特别是最小化控制努力（L1范数）所需的最优控制剖面及其性质，以及努力函数E(β,f)的数学结构。

Method: 在相平面(U, P=U_x)中通过变分法分析最优控制剖面P_β(U)，推导必要且充分的一阶最优条件，并利用反应扩散方程和行波解的结构研究努力函数的正则性与渐近性质。

Result: 1) 存在唯一的最优剖面P_β(U)；2) 满足显式的一阶最优条件（充要）；3) 控制作用在满足P_β(U)=√(U f(U))的开子集上，一致可积且关于(β,f)光滑；4) E(β,f)关于β为C1类，关于f为Lipschitz连续，且β→∞时渐近线性；5) E(β,f)关于β既不凸也不次可加。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了入侵物种控制模型中最小努力行进波的最优性结构，为大规模动力学分析提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We develop a detailed analysis of optimal traveling waves $U(t,x) = U(x - βt)$ for a model of invasive-species control proposed in [Bressan, Chiri, and Salehi, Math. Models Methods Appl. Sci., 2022] : the relative density $U \in [0,1]$ of the invasive species satisfies the following reaction-diffusion equation with a positive control \begin{equation} \label{Equa:PDE_abstract} U_t = U_{xx} + f(U) - \tilde α(t,x) U, \quad U \in [0,1], \ \tilde α\geq 0. \end{equation} The control $\tilde α(t,x)$ represents the fraction of the population removed at $(t,x)$: the minimal control effort $E(β,f)$ required to sustain a traveling invasion front with prescribed speed $β$ is defined as the minimal $L^1$-norm of $\tilde α$ for a traveling wave solution $U(x-βt)$ to the PDE. In order to study large scale dynamics $(t,x) \mapsto (εt,εx)$, a fundamental role is played by the structure of traveling waves and the convexity and regularity properties of $E$. The main results of this paper are the following: 1)In the phase plane $(U,P=U_x)$, there exists a unique optimal profile $P_β(U)$ minimizing the effort; 2) It satisfies explicit first-order conditions, which are both necessary and sufficient; 3) The associated control is acting on an open subset of the set $\{U : P_β(U) = \sqrt{U f(U)}\}$, in particular it is uniformly integrable, and it depends smoothly on $(β,f)$ on a dense open set; 4)The effort function $E(β,f)$ is only $C^1$ w.r.t. $β$ and Lipschitz w.r.t. $f$ in the $C^2$-topology, and is asymptocally linear for $β\to \infty$; 5)$β\mapsto E(β,f)$ is in general neither convex nor subadditive.

</details>


### [200] [Distributionally Robust Joint Information and Mechanism Design for Multi-Area Power System Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24015)
*Furkan Sezer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续时间随机Stackelberg控制问题，领导者通过信息结构（高斯公共信号）和转移机制（Groves转移）而非直接动力学来引导战略性追随者系统。在潜环境为跳-扩散、滤波器无限维的情况下，证明了真实披露下有效行为是占优策略最佳响应，诱导的微分博弈存在饱和与Bang-Bang纳什反馈。通过相对熵模糊性邻域，将领导者的分布鲁棒问题转化为双控制器Isaacs方程，证明激励对齐将双层Stackelberg问题简化为单层鲁棒控制问题，并刻画值函数为唯一粘性解。在2021年冬季风暴Uri校准的ERCOT多区域电力系统协调中，展示了信息设计与市场耦合的互补效应：互济消除约8%社会成本，公共信息披露使福利成本降低37%-48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究领导者如何通过非标准渠道（信息结构和转移机制）而非直接动力学控制来协调战略追随者系统，特别是在环境不确定性高、滤波器无限维的复杂设定下，为分布鲁棒性提供理论保障，并应用于极端天气下电力系统韧性协调的实际问题。

Method: 建立连续时间随机Stackelberg博弈模型；领导者设计高斯公共信号通道（投影滤波跟踪信念）和Groves转移机制；假设真实披露下有效行为为占优策略；将分布鲁棒问题转化为双控制器Isaacs方程；证明激励对齐简化问题结构；通过粘性解理论刻画值函数；在ERCOT多区域电力系统上以2021年冬季风暴Uri进行校准实验。

Result: 激励对齐将双层Stackelberg问题简化为单层鲁棒控制问题；值函数为唯一粘性解，Bang-Bang反馈非光滑处切换集为Lebesgue零测；实证显示：互济消除约8%社会成本（0.82GW联络线下），FERC/DOE推荐容量下提升至约30%；公共信息披露在自给自足下降低37%福利成本，在市场耦合下降低48%；信息设计与市场耦合在系统性风险下互补。

Conclusion: 提出的框架在理论上统一了随机Stackelberg博弈、信息设计和鲁棒控制，证明了信息通道和转移机制可有效替代直接动力学控制；在电力系统韧性协调中，信息设计与市场耦合互为补充，公共信息披露在极端天气下显著提升社会福利。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time stochastic Stackelberg control problem in which a leader steers a system of strategic followers through two non-standard channels - the information structure and a transfer mechanism - rather than through the dynamics directly. The latent environment is a jump-diffusion; the leader commits to a Gaussian public-signaling channel whose belief consequences are tracked by a finite-dimensional projection filter (the exact filter being infinite-dimensional), together with a Groves transfer that aligns the followers' incentives. Under truthful disclosure, efficient behavior is a dominant-strategy best response, and the induced differential game admits saturated and bang-bang Nash feedback. We cast the leader's distributionally robust problem, over a relative-entropy ambiguity neighborhood, as a two-controller Isaacs equation; prove that incentive alignment collapses the bilevel Stackelberg problem to a single robust control problem with an exact first-order condition; and characterize the value function as the unique viscosity solution, with a verification theorem valid for the non-smooth bang-bang feedback and a semiconcavity result that renders the switching set Lebesgue-null. We instantiate the framework on resilient multi-area power-system coordination under extreme weather. Calibrated to the 2021 Winter Storm Uri, an Isaacs solve over ERCOT's near-islanded interconnection (a 0.82 GW tie, under 2% of peak) shows mutual aid removes about 8% of social cost, rising to roughly 30% under the FERC/DOE-recommended interregional transfer capability; a reserve-scheduling experiment shows that public disclosure lowers welfare cost by 37% under autarky and 48% under market coupling, and that information design and market coupling are complements under common (systemic) risk.

</details>


### [201] [Extreme-Case Distorted Utility under Moment Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24294)
*Zehao Li,Yijie Peng,Hui Shao,Chung-Piaw Teo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究矩约束下分布鲁棒优化中极端情形扭曲效用的内层问题，通过分位数域重新建模和等距投影处理单调性约束，得到了非光滑非凸效用函数的一阶最优性条件和闭式极值分布，并应用于生成式AI推理的容量规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒决策中需要在已知矩信息的条件下评估尾部敏感的扭曲效用，但现有方法对非光滑、非凸效用函数缺乏统一的处理方法。

Method: 将问题重新表述到分位数域，利用非光滑变分分析推导最优性条件；通过等距投影技术将单调性约束转化为离散线性尺度计算，处理无限维约束。

Result: 得到了局部Lipschitz非凸非光滑效用函数在矩约束下的极值分布闭式解；通过三个例子（Scarf界的VaR扩展、GlueVaR扭曲、CVaR下的激励合约）验证了方法；在生成式AI推理容量规划中表明考虑矩模糊性可降低所需容量并保持服务合规性。

Conclusion: 所提出的内层问题刻画可直接嵌入外层鲁棒优化，为矩分布鲁棒决策提供了高效的理论工具和计算框架。

Abstract: Many operations decisions under distributional ambiguity, from pricing and inventory to capacity and contracting, evaluate an action through a tail-sensitive distorted utility of an uncertain payoff and hedge against the least favorable distribution consistent with a few known moments; the resulting worst-case evaluation is the inner problem of a moment-based distributionally robust decision. We study this inner problem, the extreme-case distorted utility under moment constraints, for a locally Lipschitz utility that may be nonsmooth and neither convex nor concave together with a general, possibly atomic, distortion. Recasting the problem in the quantile domain, we develop a unified method that yields exact first-order optimality conditions and closed-form extremal values and distributions for both the worst and best cases, drawing on nonsmooth variational analysis. A central step treats the monotonicity constraint by isotonic projection onto the monotone cone, turning an abstract infinite-dimensional restriction into an inexpensive inner solve that scales linearly in the discretization. The method recovers and extends classical moment bounds through three examples: a range value-at-risk extension of the Scarf bound, GlueVaR distortions with a reward--penalty utility, and a capped incentive contract under conditional value-at-risk. As the inner oracle of a robust min-max decision, the characterization embeds directly in outer robust optimization, illustrated on a real capacity-provisioning problem for generative artificial intelligence inference where accounting for moment ambiguity lowers required capacity while preserving service compliance.

</details>


### [202] [A multi-objective perspective on block-structured integer programs with one soft coupling constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24318)
*Mark Lyngesen,Kathrin Klamroth,Britta Efkes,Sune Lauth Gadegaard*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多目标视角下的块结构整数规划方法，通过将软耦合约束视为第二目标，将单目标问题转化为可加性双目标优化问题。利用二分搜索聚焦感兴趣区域，引入λ查找机制避免重复计算，并提出从区域中间向外扩展的算法来获取所有非支配解。计算实验表明该方法在减少整数规划调用次数上显著优于传统二分搜索，对大规模实例可作为强启发式方法并提供最优解差距界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有单个软耦合约束的块结构整数规划问题，传统方法计算完整帕累托前沿代价高昂，需要更高效的算法来提供最优解边界并减少计算开销。

Method: (1) 将软耦合约束解释为第二目标，将耦合单目标问题转化为可加性双目标优化问题；(2) 提出基于二分搜索的算法，聚焦软约束限制附近的感兴趣区域，提供单目标最优解的可证明边界；(3) 利用块结构引入λ查找机制，避免重复计算子问题；(4) 提出从感兴趣区域中间向外扩展的新算法，快速收敛于非支配解的表示。

Result: 计算研究表明该方法显著减少了整数规划调用次数，优于传统二分搜索；对于大规模实例，该方法可作为强启发式方法，提供最优解差距界限及权衡信息。

Conclusion: 通过多目标视角和区域聚焦策略，有效降低了计算复杂度；λ查找机制和中间向外扩展算法进一步提升了效率；该方法不仅高效求解，还能提供丰富的权衡信息和最优性保证。

Abstract: This paper presents a multi-objective perspective on block-structured integer programs featuring a single soft coupling constraint. By interpreting the coupling constraint as a second objective, we transform the coupled single-objective problem into an additively-separable bi-objective optimization problem. To avoid the expensive computation of the full Pareto front, we introduce an algorithm, which uses a binary search to isolate a region of interest around the soft constraint limit. This algorithm provides provable bounds on the single-objective optimum. We further enhance this algorithm, by exploiting the block-structure, using a novel $λ$-lookup mechanism to skip repeated sub-problem calculations. Finally, for scenarios requiring all non-dominated solutions within the region of interest, we propose a new approach, that works its way from the middle of the region of interest outwards. This algorithm shows quick convergence in terms of representation. Computational studies demonstrate that our methods dramatically reduce integer programming calls, thereby outperforming traditional dichotomic search. For large instances the method works as a strong heuristic providing bounds on the gap to an optimal solution, providing trade-off information in addition to the solution.

</details>


### [203] [Line Planning at Scale: Models, Methods and Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24386)
*Rolf van Lieshout,Bart van Rossum*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文比较了线路规划问题中的四种模型：换乘网络（CGN）和三种更紧凑的近似模型。实验基于荷兰和瑞士铁路网络的972个实例发现，CGN仅在小规模或简单问题上表现尚可，在大规模网络中常无法找到可行解；而紧凑的直接连接模型在超过83%的实例上表现最佳。结论认为，精心设计的近似方法比精确的换乘建模更适合大规模线路规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线路规划是公共交通规划的基础步骤，传统上使用换乘网络（CGN）精确建模乘客路径选择，但CGN规模增长过快，难以扩展到大规模问题。因此需要研究是否存在更紧凑、可扩展的替代模型，并比较它们的解质量和建模精度。

Method: 将CGN与三种紧凑模型（在换乘近似方式上不同）进行对比，为每种模型开发了最先进的求解方法，并在基于荷兰和瑞士铁路网络的972个实例上进行评估。

Result: CGN仅在小规模或简单实例上具有竞争力，在大型网络上经常无法找到可行解；紧凑的直接连接模型在超过83%的实例上取得了最佳解。

Conclusion: 对于大规模线路规划，精心设计的近似方法（而非精确的换乘建模）是更有前景的基础。

Abstract: Line planning, the problem of deciding which lines to operate and at what frequency, is a fundamental step in public transport planning. To accurately model passenger routing, the problem is traditionally defined on a change-and-go network (CGN), which captures transfers between lines exactly. However, this network grows large quickly and is hard to solve at scale. We compare the CGN against three more compact models, differing with respect to how transfers are approximated, and characterize how they relate in terms of solution quality and modeling accuracy. We develop state-of-the-art solution methods tailored to each model, and evaluate all four across 972 instances based on the Dutch and Swiss railway networks. Contrary to the CGN's canonical status, we find that it is competitive only on small or easy instances and often fails to find any feasible solution on large networks. Instead, a compact direct connection model performs best overall, finding the best solution on over 83\% of instances. Our results indicate that carefully designed approximations, rather than exact transfer modeling, are the more promising foundation for large-scale line planning.

</details>


### [204] [Trade-off invariance for weighted scalarizations in multi-objective optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24493)
*Jona Klemenc,Alessandro Scagliotti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究抽象多目标最小化问题的加权和标量化，在不假设拓扑、凸性、紧性或下半连续性的条件下，证明了标量化的权衡不变原理在目标空间中产生一个通有唯一性性质：对于几乎每个权重向量，所有相应的加权和标量化的最小化者都具有相同的目标向量；此外，排除零测子集后，所有最小化序列都确定相同的极限目标向量。几何解释为：对于几乎每个正权重向量，标量化至多暴露一个非支配点，且最小化序列在可达集的闭包中至多确定一个渐近暴露的目标向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多目标优化中，加权和标量化是最常用的方法之一，但传统理论通常需要凸性、紧性等假设。本文旨在最一般的抽象设定（无拓扑、凸性等假设）下，研究加权和标量化能否保证解的唯一性性质。

Method: 以开单纯形作为正权重的参数空间，利用权衡不变原理（Trade-off Invariance Principle）作为分析工具，在无任何拓扑或凸性假设的抽象多目标最小化问题中，研究加权和标量化的通有性质。

Result: 证明了：(1) 对于几乎每个权重向量，所有对应的加权和标量化最小化者具有相同的目标向量；(2) 除去一个零测子集后，所有最小化序列确定相同的极限目标向量，与序列选择无关。几何上，(3) 对于几乎每个正权重向量，标量化至多暴露一个非支配点；(4) 最小化序列在可达集的闭包中至多确定一个渐近暴露的目标向量。

Conclusion: 在最一般的抽象多目标优化框架下，加权和标量化具有通有的唯一性性质，为多目标优化中加权和方法的理论合理性提供了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: We consider weighted-sum scalarizations for an abstract multi-objective minimization problem defined by the vector-valued map $U\ni u\mapsto ( f_1(u),\ldots, f_N(u))$, where $U$ is an arbitrary nonempty set and no topology, convexity, compactness, or lower semicontinuity assumption is imposed. Using the open simplex as parameter space for positive weights, we show that the Trade-off Invariance Principle for scalarizations yields a generic uniqueness property in the objective space. Namely, for almost every weight vector, all minimizers of the corresponding weighted-sum scalarization have the same objective vector. Moreover, excluding again a null-measure subset, all minimizing sequences determine the same limiting objective vector, independently of the chosen sequence. We also give a geometric interpretation of these results in the attainable objective set: for almost every positive weight vector, the scalarization exposes at most one nondominated point. Moreover, minimizing sequences determine at most one asymptotically exposed objective vector in the closure of the attainable set.

</details>


### [205] [Variational convexity: new characterizations, calculus rules, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24545)
*Radu Ioan Bot,Ziyuan Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了变分凸函数的刻画、保持运算及其在非线性规划中的应用，提供了通过近端包络和上图来刻画变分凸性的方法，并证明了非线性组合、线性组合、求和及近端平均等运算能保持变分凸性。进一步将这些结果应用于具有非光滑目标函数、连续可微不等式约束和仿射等式约束的非线性规划问题，得到了确保局部极小点（而非仅仅是驻点）的新条件，超越了现有要求目标函数和约束至少二次连续可微的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分凸性是Rockafellar于2019年提出的广义凸性概念，它能保证非凸优化问题的驻点具有局部最优性。本文旨在建立变分凸函数的刻画定理，研究其保持运算，并将这些理论结果应用于更一般的非线性规划问题，以得到比现有结果更宽松的局部极小点充分条件。

Method: 通过近端包络和上图对变分凸函数进行刻画；研究非线性组合、线性组合、求和、近端平均等运算对变分凸性的保持性质；将上述结果应用于含非光滑目标、连续可微不等式约束和仿射等式约束的非线性规划问题。

Result: 得到了变分凸函数通过近端包络和上图的完整刻画；证明了非线性组合、线性组合、求和及近端平均等运算保持变分凸性；在更宽松的条件下（非光滑目标、一次连续可微约束）给出了确保局部极小点的充分条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了变分凸函数的理论和运算性质，并将其成功应用于非线性规划，大幅放宽了经典结果中对目标函数和约束的光滑性要求，为更广泛的非凸优化问题提供了局部最优性保证。

Abstract: Introduced by R.T. Rockafellar in 2019, variational convexity is a generalized notion of convexity under which stationary points of nonconvex optimization problems can still be guaranteed to exhibit local optimality. In this paper, we provide characterizations of variationally convex functions through their proximal hulls and epigraphs, and investigate operations that preserve variational convexity, including nonlinear and linear composition, summation, and proximal averaging. We further apply these results to identify variational convexity in nonlinear programming problems with possibly nonsmooth objectives, continuously differentiable inequality constraints, and affine equality constraints. This leads to new conditions ensuring local minimizers, rather than merely stationary points, for such problems, extending beyond current state-of-the-art results that typically require twice continuously differentiable objectives and constraints.

</details>


### [206] [Decomposition-Based Constructive Heuristics for the Linear Ordering Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24558)
*Kazutoshi Ando,Tatsuya Sugimoto,Noriyoshi Sukegawa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对线性排序问题（LOP），提出了一种统一的递归框架，将索引集启发式划分为更小的子集，精确求解足够小的子问题，并拼接所得排列。在该框架下研究了三种递归划分原则：基于强连通分量的层级图方法、基于割的最小割方法以及基于分数的递归Borda方法。实验表明递归Borda方法在平均解质量上最优，但经插入局部搜索后各方法差异显著缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性排序问题（LOP）是经典的NP难组合优化问题，现有构造式启发式方法在解质量上仍存在改进空间，亟需系统性的递归框架来统一和提升构造式启发式的性能。

Method: 提出统一递归框架，通过启发式划分索引集、精确求解子问题并拼接排列。在该框架下分别实现基于强连通分量的层级图方法、基于最小割的划分方法以及基于Borda分数的递归Borda方法，并理论分析了各方法的Condorcet性质。

Result: 递归Borda方法满足Condorcet准则而Borda规则不满足；层级图法和最小割法满足更强的Condorcet型性质。xLOLIB实例实验表明递归Borda方法平均解质量最优，但插入局部搜索后各方法最终解质量差异显著缩小。

Conclusion: 递归框架能有效提升构造式启发式性能，其中递归Borda方法在无局部搜索时表现最佳，但局部搜索可弥合各方法间的质量差距。

Abstract: The linear ordering problem (LOP) is a classical NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem. In this paper, we study constructive heuristics for the LOP. We propose a unified recursive framework in which the index set is heuristically partitioned into smaller subsets, sufficiently small subproblems are solved exactly, and the resulting permutations are concatenated. Within this framework, we investigate three different recursive partitioning principles: a rule based on strongly connected components (the level graph method), a cut-based rule (the minimum cut method), and a score-based rule (the recursive Borda method). We first show that the recursive Borda method satisfies the Condorcet criterion, whereas the Borda rule does not. The level graph method and the minimum cut method satisfy a stronger Condorcet-type property, which the recursive Borda method does not satisfy. Computational experiments on xLOLIB instances show that the recursive Borda method provides the best average solution quality among the tested constructive heuristics. They also show that, after applying insertion-based local search, the differences in final solution quality among these heuristics become much smaller.

</details>


### [207] [Bridging Identification and Second-Order Acceleration: A Fast Alternating Minimization Framework for Composite Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24600)
*Zihao Xia,Min Tao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种交替最小化框架，融合近端梯度步骤和限制在动态低维子空间上的立方正则化牛顿更新，用于求解含光滑函数和可能非凸非光滑正则化子的复合优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含非凸非光滑正则化子的复合优化问题，现有方法在全局收敛性、有限识别性质和收敛速度方面存在不足，需要一种能同时兼顾这些性质的高效算法。

Method: 提出交替最小化框架，集成近端梯度步骤与立方正则化牛顿更新，并将后者限制在动态识别的低维子空间上；利用KL性质和KL指数指导自适应阈值策略。

Result: 在KL性质下建立了全局收敛性；不依赖非退化假设证明了有限识别性质；达到了𝒪(ε^{-3/2})的迭代复杂度以实现近似二阶平稳性；数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在理论保证（全局收敛、有限识别、复杂度）和实际性能上均表现优异，为求解非凸非光滑复合优化问题提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: We consider a class of composite optimization problems involving a smooth function and a proper, lower semicontinuous regularizer, which may be nonconvex and nonsmooth.
  We propose a novel alternating minimization framework that integrates proximal-gradient steps with cubic-regularized Newton updates restricted to a dynamically identified low-dimensional subspace.
  Under the Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz (KL) property, we establish global convergence of the proposed method to a stationary point.
  Moreover, by incorporating an adaptive thresholding strategy guided by the KL exponent, we prove a finite identification property without imposing any nondegeneracy assumptions.
  We further develop a local convergence analysis and show that the proposed method attains a worst-case iteration complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ for achieving approximate second-order stationarity.
  Numerical experiments on both synthetic and real datasets demonstrate the efficiency and effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [208] [Parallel Dynamic Programming for Conic Linear Quadratic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24632)
*Luyao Zhang,Gabriel Bravo-Palacios,Brian Plancher,Sergio Grammatico*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于ADMM的并行时间域分解方法，将LQ最优控制问题沿时间轴拆解，利用动态规划推导出Riccati递归的变体来并行求解子问题，在多核CPU上实现了相比现有方法高达5倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的LQ控制问题求解方法本质上是串行的，尽管现代多核CPU拥有强大的并行计算能力，但实时可扩展性仍然受限。为了充分利用并行计算资源，需要设计并行化求解策略。

Method: 采用"分而治之"策略，将ADMM框架下的内层原始更新表述为LQ问题，并沿时间轴拆分该问题；利用动态规划推导出Riccati递归的变体，使每个子问题可以并行求解。

Result: 在两个实际应用场景的数值基准测试中，相较现有方法，在多核CPU硬件上实现了高达5倍的加速比。

Conclusion: 所提出的并行时间域分解方法能够有效加速LQ控制问题的求解，为实时MPC等在多核平台上的可扩展性提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic (LQ) control problems are at the heart of linear control theory and Model Predictive Control (MPC). While performant, standard approaches to solving such problems are inherently serial, limiting real-time scalability despite the parallel computing power available on modern multi-core CPUs. Contributing to addressing this challenge and motivated by ``divide and conquer'' strategies, we present a parallel-in-time approach that solves computationally demanding conic optimal control problems through the use of the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). In particular, we formulate the inner primal update of ADMM as an LQ problem and split the reformulated problem along the time horizon. This enables us to derive a variant of the Riccati recursion using dynamic programming to solve each subproblem in parallel. Numerical benchmarks on two real-world applications demonstrate as much as a 5x speedup compared to existing related approaches on multi-core CPU hardware.

</details>


### [209] [Suboptimal and Reduced-Order MPC via Timescale Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24641)
*Stefano Di Gregorio,Guido Carnevale,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用框架，用于设计和分析次优和降阶非线性模型预测控制（MPC）架构，通过有限次迭代优化和降阶模型实现实时运行，并利用时间尺度分离理论证明在足够快采样率下闭环系统的递归可行性和指数稳定性，在MujoCo仿真中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非线性MPC在实际应用中面临计算负担重和模型复杂度高的挑战，需要一种能够在实时操作中平衡优化误差和模型失配的通用框架。

Method: (i) 通过运行通用最优控制算法有限次迭代来次优地计算控制动作；(ii) 采用忽略部分植物动力学的降阶模型；(iii) 将采样时间作为可调设计参数；(iv) 利用时间尺度分离理论分析闭环系统。

Result: 证明了在足够快采样率下，闭环系统保持递归可行性并实现指数稳定的平衡点；在欠驱动双连杆机械臂的MuJoCo高保真物理引擎仿真中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为处理次优优化和模型降阶带来的耦合问题提供了理论保障，通过合理设计采样时间可确保闭环稳定性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a generalized framework for the design and analysis of suboptimal and reduced-order nonlinear Model Predictive Control (MPC) architectures. The proposed framework manages real-time operation of MPC schemes by (i) computing the control action suboptimally, i.e., by running a generic optimal control algorithm for a finite number of iterations, and (ii) relying on a reduced-order model that neglects part of the plant dynamics (accounting for, e.g., unmodeled dynamics or a low-level compensator). To rigorously handle the interplay between optimization error and model mismatch, we treat the sampling time as a tunable design parameter. We analyze the resulting closed-loop system, comprising the full-order physical plant interconnected with the iterative optimization algorithm (treated as a dynamical system), by leveraging tools from timescale separation. We prove that operating at a sufficiently fast sampling rate ensures that the closed-loop system maintains recursive feasibility and achieves an exponentially stable equilibrium point. The effectiveness of the proposed framework is validated on an underactuated two-link robotic arm through virtual experiments in the high-fidelity MuJoCo physics engine.

</details>


### [210] [Observer design for Lur'e systems via injection of a reconstructed nonlinear output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24656)
*Adel Malik Annabi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对Lur'e系统观测器设计中增益随耦合强度过度增长的问题，提出通过二阶滑模观测器重构非线性项并将其注入Luenberger观测器作为额外测量，形成双通道校正机制，在保证收敛性的同时维持适中增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Lur'e系统观测器设计在特定参数下观测器增益会随耦合强度无界增长，导致实际应用中增益过大而不可行，需要一种在强非线性耦合下仍能保持适中增益的观测器设计方法。

Method: 采用一组二阶滑模观测器重构关键非线性项，将其作为额外测量注入Luenberger观测器，形成第二条校正通道以衰减误差动态中的非线性耦合；给出了包含经典LMI为新特例的新LMI条件保证收敛性。

Result: 展示了经典设计需要过大增益而所提设计保持适中增益的参数区间；通过Wilson-Cowan网络实例说明了非线性耦合强度、观测器增益和噪声之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提观测器在强非线性耦合场景下有效解决了经典LMI设计增益过大的问题，并清晰刻画了两种设计间的权衡以及噪声条件下的适用性。

Abstract: Observer design for Lur'e systems typically reduces to solving a linear matrix inequality (LMI). In certain cases, the observer gain may grow unbounded with the coupling strength. We propose reconstructing key nonlinear terms via a bank of second-order sliding-mode observers and injecting them into a Luenberger observer as additional measurements. This feeds a second correction channel that attenuates the nonlinear coupling in the error dynamics. The convergence of the combined observer is guaranteed under a new LMI which contains the classical one as a special case. We exhibit parameter regimes where classical designs require impractically large gains, while the proposed design maintains moderate gains. A stability analysis of the proposed observer characterizes the trade-off between the two designs and identifies the noise regimes in which the proposed design is preferable. We illustrate the approach on a Wilson-Cowan network showing the trade-off between the nonlinear coupling strength, observer gain, and noise.

</details>


### [211] [New Bounds for the Last Iterate of the Stochastic subGradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24879)
*Guglielmo Beretta,Tommaso Cesari,Roberto Colomboni,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 研究一维凸Lipschitz目标函数下随机次梯度方法（SsGM）的最后迭代点收敛性。证明在i.i.d.次梯度噪声下，固定步长η=Θ(1/√n)时最后迭代点优化误差为O(1/√n)，去掉了现有理论中的log n因子；但若无i.i.d.假设仅具有界方差，误差可达到O(log n/√n)，表明此时最后迭代点即使在一维情况下也是次优的，否定了Koren和Segal (COLT 2020)提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除随机次梯度方法最后迭代点收敛性分析中多余的log n因子，并回答Koren和Segal (COLT 2020)提出的公开问题：在仅具有界方差假设下，一维情形中最后迭代点是否最优。

Method: 理论分析：针对一维凸Lipschitz目标函数，分别考虑i.i.d.次梯度噪声和仅具有界方差的噪声两种情形，对固定步长η=Θ(1/√n)策略下最后迭代点的优化误差进行上界和下界分析。

Result: i.i.d.噪声下最后迭代点误差为O(1/√n)，去掉了log n因子；仅具有界方差假设下误差下界为Ω(log n/√n)，表明此时最后迭代点次优。

Conclusion: 在i.i.d.噪声假设下，随机次梯度方法的最后迭代点可达到最优收敛率；但在更弱的仅具有界方差假设下，即使在一维情形中最后迭代点也是次优的，否定了相关公开问题。

Abstract: We study the last iterate of the stochastic subgradient method for one-dimensional convex Lipschitz objectives. For a fixed horizon $n$, we consider the standard fixed stepsizes $η=Θ(1/\sqrt n)$. We prove that, for such stepsize policies, under additive i.i.d. subgradient noise with uniformly bounded variance, the last iterate features an optimization error of order $1/\sqrt n$, thereby removing the extra $(\log n)$ factor present in existing generic bounds. On the other hand, we show that without the i.i.d. assumption, the optimization error can be of order $(\log n)/\sqrt n$. Thus, under the uniformly bounded variance assumption alone, the last iterate of SsGM is suboptimal even in dimension one, resolving negatively an open problem posed in Koren and Segal, COLT, 2020.

</details>
